249 |     private SourcePosition<AliasTransformation> getSourceRegion(Node n) {
250 |       Node testNode = n;
251 |       Node next = null;
252 |       for (; next != null || testNode.isScript();) {
253 |         next = testNode.getNext();
254 |         testNode = testNode.getParent();
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       int endLine = next == null ? Integer.MAX_VALUE : next.getLineno();
258 |       int endChar = next == null ? Integer.MAX_VALUE : next.getCharno();
259 |       SourcePosition<AliasTransformation> pos =
260 |           new SourcePosition<AliasTransformation>() {};
261 |       pos.setPositionInformation(
262 |           n.getLineno(), n.getCharno(), endLine, endChar);
263 |       return pos;
264 |     }

413 |   private void checkFirstModule(List<JSModule> modules) {
414 |     if (modules.isEmpty()) {

416 |     } else if (modules.get(0).getInputs().isEmpty() && modules.size() > 1) {
417 |       // The root module may only be empty if there is exactly 1 module.

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 322-388
Documentation:

Method code:
322 |     @Override
323 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
324 |       if (isCallToScopeMethod(n)) {
325 |         validateScopeCall(t, n, n.getParent());
326 |       }
327 | 
328 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() < 2) {
329 |         return;
330 |       }
331 | 
332 |       int type = n.getType();
333 |       Var aliasVar = null;
334 |       if (type == Token.NAME) {
335 |         String name = n.getString();
336 |         Var lexicalVar = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
337 |         if (lexicalVar != null && lexicalVar == aliases.get(name)) {
338 |           aliasVar = lexicalVar;
339 |         }
340 |       }
341 | 
342 |       // Validate the top-level of the goog.scope block.
343 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() == 2) {
344 |         if (aliasVar != null && NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
345 |           if (aliasVar.getNode() == n) {
346 |             aliasDefinitionsInOrder.add(n);
347 | 
348 |             // Return early, to ensure that we don't record a definition
349 |             // twice.
350 |             return;
351 |           } else {
352 |             report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_ALIAS_REDEFINED, n.getString());
353 |           }
354 |         }
355 | 
356 |         if (type == Token.RETURN) {
357 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_RETURN);
358 |         } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
359 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_REFERENCES_THIS);
360 |         } else if (type == Token.THROW) {
361 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_THROW);
362 |         }
363 |       }
364 | 
365 |       // Validate all descendent scopes of the goog.scope block.
366 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() >= 2) {
367 |         // Check if this name points to an alias.
368 |         if (aliasVar != null) {
369 |           // Note, to support the transitive case, it's important we don't
370 |           // clone aliasedNode here.  For example,
371 |           // var g = goog; var d = g.dom; d.createElement('DIV');
372 |           // The node in aliasedNode (which is "g") will be replaced in the
373 |           // changes pass above with "goog".  If we cloned here, we'd end up
374 |           // with <code>g.dom.createElement('DIV')</code>.
375 |           Node aliasedNode = aliasVar.getInitialValue();
376 |           aliasUsages.add(new AliasedNode(n, aliasedNode));
377 |         }
378 | 
379 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
380 |         if (info != null) {
381 |           for (Node node : info.getTypeNodes()) {
382 |             fixTypeNode(node);
383 |           }
384 |         }
385 | 
386 |         // TODO(robbyw): Error for goog.scope not at root.
387 |       }
388 |     }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }

602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {

605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.

1706 |   public Node cloneTree() {

1720 |   }

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 246-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
246 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
247 |     this.options = options;
248 |     if (errorManager == null) {
249 |       if (outStream == null) {
250 |         setErrorManager(
251 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
252 |       } else {
253 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
254 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
255 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
256 |         setErrorManager(printer);
257 |       }
258 |     }
259 | 
260 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
261 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
262 |       options.checkTypes = true;
263 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
264 |       options.checkTypes = false;
265 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
266 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
267 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
268 |       // parser type warnings.
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
271 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
272 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
276 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
277 |       options.setWarningLevel(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
279 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
283 |       options.setWarningLevel(
284 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
285 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
289 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
290 |     guards.add(
291 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
292 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
293 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
294 | 
295 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
296 | 
297 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
298 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
299 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
300 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
301 |     // error off.
302 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
303 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
304 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
305 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
306 |     }
307 | 
308 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
309 |   }

88 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
89 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3005-3031
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3005 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3006 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3007 |     if (info == null) {
3008 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3009 |       if (parent == null) {
3010 |         return null;
3011 |       }
3012 | 
3013 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3014 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3015 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3016 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3017 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3018 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3020 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3022 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3024 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3025 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3026 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3027 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3028 |       }
3029 |     }
3030 |     return info;
3031 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
479 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
480 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
481 |          current != null;
482 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
483 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
484 |         return true;
485 |       }
486 |     }
487 |     return false;
488 |   }

===== M0096 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0155 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 499-519
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
499 |   @Override
500 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
501 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
502 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
503 |     if (unknown) {
504 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
505 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
506 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
507 |         unknown = false;
508 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
509 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
510 |             unknown = true;
511 |             break;
512 |           }
513 |         }
514 |       } else {
515 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
516 |       }
517 |     }
518 |     return unknown;
519 |   }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1224-1235
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1224 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1225 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1226 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1227 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1228 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1229 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1230 |       } else {
1231 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1232 |       }
1233 |     }
1234 |     return builder.build();
1235 |   }

===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 458-474
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
458 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
459 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
460 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
461 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
462 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
463 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
464 |         String inputName = input.getName();
465 | 
466 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
467 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
468 |         // will get an error down the line.
469 |         inputs.add(input);
470 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
471 |       }
472 |     }
473 |     return inputs;
474 |   }

===== M0177 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

===== M0193 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1820-1837
Documentation:
/**
   * Merge a block with its parent block.
   * @return Whether the block was removed.
   */
Method code:
1820 |   static boolean tryMergeBlock(Node block) {
1821 |     Preconditions.checkState(block.isBlock());
1822 |     Node parent = block.getParent();
1823 |     // Try to remove the block if its parent is a block/script or if its
1824 |     // parent is label and it has exactly one child.
1825 |     if (isStatementBlock(parent)) {
1826 |       Node previous = block;
1827 |       while (block.hasChildren()) {
1828 |         Node child = block.removeFirstChild();
1829 |         parent.addChildAfter(child, previous);
1830 |         previous = child;
1831 |       }
1832 |       parent.removeChild(block);
1833 |       return true;
1834 |     } else {
1835 |       return false;
1836 |     }
1837 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args

102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1567-1581
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1567 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1568 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1569 |       return getString();
1570 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1571 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1572 |       if (left == null) {
1573 |         return null;
1574 |       }
1575 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1576 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1577 |       return "this";
1578 |     } else {
1579 |       return null;
1580 |     }
1581 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0223 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 341-367
Documentation:

Method code:
341 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
342 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
343 |     // two cases:
344 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
345 |     // and
346 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
347 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
348 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
349 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
350 |     //
351 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
352 |     //
353 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
354 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
355 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
356 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
357 |     //
358 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
359 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
360 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
361 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
362 | 
363 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
364 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
365 |     }
366 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
367 |   }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 794-807
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

===== M0235 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();

239 |         }

241 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {

===== M0263 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0280 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 910-937
Documentation:

Method code:
910 |     @Override
911 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
912 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
913 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
914 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
915 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
916 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
917 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
918 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
919 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
920 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
921 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
922 |         //
923 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
924 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
925 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
926 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
927 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
928 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
929 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
930 |             (sourceString.substring(
931 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
932 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
933 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 |       return n;
937 |     }

===== M0281 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

Method code:
93 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
94 |     switch (n.getType()) {
95 |       case Token.LABEL:

97 |         return;
98 |       case Token.BLOCK:
99 |         validateBlock(n);
100 |         return;
101 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
102 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
103 |         return;
104 |       case Token.WITH:
105 |         validateWith(n);
106 |         return;
107 |       case Token.FOR:
108 |         validateFor(n);
109 |         return;
110 |       case Token.WHILE:
111 |         validateWhile(n);
112 |         return;
113 |       case Token.DO:
114 |         validateDo(n);
115 |         return;
116 |       case Token.SWITCH:
117 |         validateSwitch(n);
118 |         return;
119 |       case Token.IF:
120 |         validateIf(n);
121 |         return;
122 |       case Token.VAR:
123 |         validateVar(n);
124 |         return;
125 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
126 |         validateExprStmt(n);
127 |         return;
128 |       case Token.RETURN:
129 |         validateReturn(n);
130 |         return;
131 |       case Token.THROW:
132 |         validateThrow(n);
133 |         return;
134 |       case Token.TRY:
135 |         validateTry(n);
136 |         return;
137 |       case Token.BREAK:
138 |         validateBreak(n);
139 |         return;
140 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
141 |         validateContinue(n);
142 |         return;
143 |       case Token.EMPTY:
144 |         validateChildless(n);
145 |         return;
146 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
147 |         validateChildless(n);
148 |         return;
149 |       default:
150 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
151 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
152 |     }
153 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1240-1363
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1240 |   Node parseInputs() {
1241 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1242 | 
1243 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1244 |     // individual file parse trees.
1245 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1246 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1247 |     }
1248 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1249 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1250 |     }
1251 | 
1252 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1253 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1254 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1255 | 
1256 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1257 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1258 | 
1259 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1260 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1261 | 
1262 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1263 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1264 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1265 |     }
1266 | 
1267 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1268 | 
1269 |     try {
1270 |       // Parse externs sources.
1271 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1272 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1273 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1274 |           return null;
1275 |         }
1276 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1277 |       }
1278 | 
1279 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1280 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1281 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1282 |       }
1283 | 
1284 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1285 | 
1286 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1287 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1288 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1289 |           options.closurePass) {
1290 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1291 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1292 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1293 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1294 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1295 |           }
1296 |         }
1297 | 
1298 |         try {
1299 |           inputs =
1300 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1301 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1302 |           staleInputs = true;
1303 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1304 |           report(JSError.make(
1305 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1306 | 
1307 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1308 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1309 |             return null;
1310 |           }
1311 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1312 |           report(JSError.make(
1313 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1314 | 
1315 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1316 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1317 |             return null;
1318 |           }
1319 |         }
1320 |       }
1321 | 
1322 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1323 | 
1324 |       if (staleInputs) {
1325 |         repartitionInputs();
1326 |       }
1327 | 
1328 |       // Build the AST.
1329 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1330 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1331 |         if (n == null) {
1332 |           continue;
1333 |         }
1334 | 
1335 |         if (devMode) {
1336 |           runSanityCheck();
1337 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1338 |             return null;
1339 |           }
1340 |         }
1341 | 
1342 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1343 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1344 | 
1345 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1346 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1347 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1348 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1349 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1350 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1351 |         }
1352 | 
1353 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1354 |       }
1355 | 
1356 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1357 |         return null;
1358 |       }
1359 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1360 |     } finally {
1361 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1362 |     }
1363 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 430-448
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the function's name. This method recognizes five forms:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code function name() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * </ul>
   * In two last cases with named function expressions, the second name is
   * returned (the variable of qualified name).
   *
   * @param n a node whose type is {@link Token#FUNCTION}
   * @return the function's name, or {@code null} if it has no name
   */
Method code:
430 |   static String getFunctionName(Node n) {
431 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
432 |     String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
433 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
434 |       case Token.NAME:
435 |         // var name = function() ...
436 |         // var name2 = function name1() ...
437 |         return parent.getString();
438 | 
439 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
440 |         // qualified.name = function() ...
441 |         // qualified.name2 = function name1() ...
442 |         return parent.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
443 | 
444 |       default:
445 |         // function name() ...
446 |         return name != null && name.length() != 0 ? name : null;
447 |     }
448 |   }

===== M0310 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

272 |     private void findAliases(NodeTraversal t) {
273 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
274 |       for (Var v : scope.getVarIterable()) {
275 |         Node n = v.getNode();
276 |         int type = n.getType();

===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.validateScopeCall(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 299-320
Documentation:

Method code:
299 |     private void validateScopeCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
300 |       if (preprocessorSymbolTable != null) {
301 |         preprocessorSymbolTable.addReference(n.getFirstChild());
302 |       }
303 |       if (!parent.isExprResult()) {
304 |         report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USED_IMPROPERLY);
305 |       }
306 |       if (n.getChildCount() != 2) {
307 |         // The goog.scope call should have exactly 1 parameter.  The first
308 |         // child is the "goog.scope" and the second should be the parameter.
309 |         report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_HAS_BAD_PARAMETERS);
310 |       } else {
311 |         Node anonymousFnNode = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
312 |         if (!anonymousFnNode.isFunction() ||
313 |             NodeUtil.getFunctionName(anonymousFnNode) != null ||
314 |             NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(anonymousFnNode).hasChildren()) {
315 |           report(t, anonymousFnNode, GOOG_SCOPE_HAS_BAD_PARAMETERS);
316 |         } else {
317 |           scopeCalls.add(n);

   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */

308 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
309 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field

312 |       if (refName == null) {

===== M0332 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.hotSwapScript(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 103-142
Documentation:

Method code:
103 |   @Override
104 |   public void hotSwapScript(Node root, Node originalRoot) {
105 |     Traversal traversal = new Traversal();
106 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, traversal);
107 | 
108 |     if (!traversal.hasErrors()) {
109 | 
110 |       // Apply the aliases.
111 |       for (AliasUsage aliasUsage : traversal.getAliasUsages()) {
112 |         aliasUsage.applyAlias();
113 |       }
114 | 
115 |       // Remove the alias definitions.
116 |       for (Node aliasDefinition : traversal.getAliasDefinitionsInOrder()) {
117 |         if (aliasDefinition.getParent().isVar() &&
118 |             aliasDefinition.getParent().hasOneChild()) {
119 |           aliasDefinition.getParent().detachFromParent();
120 |         } else {
121 |           aliasDefinition.detachFromParent();
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 | 
125 |       // Collapse the scopes.
126 |       for (Node scopeCall : traversal.getScopeCalls()) {
127 |         Node expressionWithScopeCall = scopeCall.getParent();
128 |         Node scopeClosureBlock = scopeCall.getLastChild().getLastChild();
129 |         scopeClosureBlock.detachFromParent();
130 |         expressionWithScopeCall.getParent().replaceChild(
131 |             expressionWithScopeCall,
132 |             scopeClosureBlock);
133 |         NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(scopeClosureBlock);
134 |       }
135 | 
136 |       if (traversal.getAliasUsages().size() > 0 ||
137 |           traversal.getAliasDefinitionsInOrder().size() > 0 ||
138 |           traversal.getScopeCalls().size() > 0) {
139 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
140 |       }
141 |     }
142 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0346 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1905-1907
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1905 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1906 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1907 |   }

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0366 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {

220 |         return;
221 |

224 |         return;

227 |       case Token.STRING:

229 |         return;

233 |         return;

Method code:
323 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
324 |     if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
325 |       // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now. This avoids
326 |       // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
327 |       // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
328 |       int lineno = node.getLineno();
329 |       irNode.setLineno(lineno);
330 |       int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
331 |       irNode.setCharno(charno);
332 |       maybeSetLengthFrom(irNode, node);
333 |     }
334 |   }

===== M0375 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0386 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 409-422
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
409 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
410 |       Node ret;
411 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
412 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
413 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
414 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
415 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
416 |       } else {
417 |         ret = transform(n);
418 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
419 |       }
420 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
421 |       return ret;
422 |     }

631 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
632 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
633 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
634 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
635 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0422 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 374-417
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
374 |   boolean setPrototype(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
375 |     if (prototype == null) {
376 |       return false;
377 |     }
378 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
379 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 | 
383 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
384 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
385 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
386 | 
387 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
388 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
389 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
390 | 
391 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
392 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
393 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
394 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
395 |     }
396 | 
397 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
398 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
399 |       if (superClass != null) {
400 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       if (isInterface()) {
404 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
405 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
406 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
407 |           }
408 |         }
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
413 |       clearCachedValues();
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     return true;
417 |   }

Method code:
87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
88 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
89 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
90 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
91 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
95 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
96 |     } else {
97 |       numberOfAdds++;
98 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
99 |       guards.remove(guard);
100 |       guards.add(guard);
101 |     }
102 |   }

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

Original range: 2814-2829

/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2814 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2815 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
2816 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2817 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2818 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2819 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2820 |       if (parent.isAssign()) {
2821 |         // on ASSIGNs
2822 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2823 |       } else if (parent.isName()) {
2824 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2825 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2826 |       }
2827 |     }
2828 |     return fnInfo;
2829 |   }

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0475 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

===== M0501 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2024-2036
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as syntactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2024 |   static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2025 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2026 |         n.isGetElem());
2027 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2028 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2029 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2030 |         || parent.isVar()
2031 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2032 |         || parent.isDec()
2033 |         || parent.isInc()
2034 |         || parent.isParamList()
2035 |         || parent.isCatch();
2036 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
270 |   private JSDocInfo handleJsDoc(AstNode node, Node irNode) {
271 |     Comment comment = node.getJsDocNode();
272 |     if (comment != null) {
273 |       JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser = createJsDocInfoParser(comment, irNode);
274 |       parsedComments.add(comment);
275 |       if (!handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(jsDocParser)) {
276 |         return jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
277 |       }
278 |     }
279 |     return null;
280 |   }

===== M0527 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.addChildrenAfter(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 670-694
Documentation:
/**
   * Add all children after 'node'.
   */
Method code:
670 |   public void addChildrenAfter(Node children, Node node) {
671 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node == null || node.parent == this);
672 |     for (Node child = children; child != null; child = child.next) {
673 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(child.parent == null);
674 |       child.parent = this;
675 |     }
676 | 
677 |     Node lastSibling = children.getLastSibling();
678 |     if (node != null) {
679 |       Node oldNext = node.next;
680 |       node.next = children;
681 |       lastSibling.next = oldNext;
682 |       if (node == last) {
683 |         last = lastSibling;
684 |       }
685 |     } else {
686 |       // Append to the beginning.
687 |       if (first != null) {
688 |         lastSibling.next = first;
689 |       } else {
690 |         last = lastSibling;
691 |       }
692 |       first = children;
693 |     }
694 |   }

===== M0529 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }
